- **read-file-at**: Read a file's **bytes as of a revision** — the structured equivalent of `git show &lt;rev&gt;:&lt;path&gt;` / `git cat-file blob &lt;rev&gt;:&lt;path&gt;`. Collapses the resolve → peel → descend → read dance into one call and guarantees the addressed object is a **blob**, returning the file's content plus its tree-entry metadata (oid + mode). For `crew &lt;group&gt; ...` it loads `lib/commands/&lt;group&gt;.js` on demand, so adding a group never touches the dispatcher. Files starting with `_` and non-`.js` files are ignored. A module that throws on load only hides itself. ([ynyrworks/crewgate](https://github.com/ynyrworks/crewgate/blob/HEAD/plugins/crewgate/lib/commands/README.md) · ★3)

- **README**: Claude Code's TUI maps every `.md` file under a plugin's `commands/` directory to a slash command. The filename, minus the `.md` extension, is the slash invocation: `commands/reflect.md` becomes `/reflect`. The frontmatter declares the command's metadata (description, argument hint, allowed-tools scope); the body becomes the prompt Claude reads when the user runs the command. - **README**: One page per command: what it does, its keywords, examples that run, what it prints and records, and its refusals. The one-line summary of all of them is in the [reference](../reference.md#verbs); `pkg HELP` prints it. ([jonx/aros-pkg](https://github.com/jonx/aros-pkg/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: **Total Commands:** 119 across 24 categories ([jr-mccoy/prompt-agent-engineering](https://github.com/jr-mccoy/prompt-agent-engineering/blob/HEAD/domain-agentic-resources/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: This toolkit ships **no** custom slash-command markdown files (those would live under `~/.claude/commands/`); built-in commands (e.g. `/model`, see `../USAGE.md`) cover day-to-day slash usage. What lives here instead are reusable **command-style PowerShell scripts** for project automation, kept so they can be dropped into any repo's `.claude/scripts/`. ([Kaidanov/grekai-skills-4all](https://github.com/Kaidanov/grekai-skills-4all/blob/HEAD/skills/my-claude-toolkit/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: `kavach-implement.md` is the `/kavach-implement` command: contract-first feature work with `kavach contract` (AGENTS.md, "Building a feature"). It is plain Markdown with `$ARGUMENTS`, so it works as a command in agents that read Markdown commands: ([kavachlabs/kavach](https://github.com/kavachlabs/kavach/blob/HEAD/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: Everything you can do in githq from the keyboard or from the command menu (⌘K) is a command: merge a pull request, open an item on GitHub, assign someone, go to the next row. This design gives each command a single definition, written once in one place: its name, icon and default key. A screen that offers the command says only what it does there and when it is available. The command menu, the key ([kedrzu/githq](https://github.com/kedrzu/githq/blob/HEAD/docs/design/commands/README.md) · ★2)
- **README**: The `shell.run` kernel tool: runs a project program (`npm test`, `cargo check`) or a skill script on the model's behalf, in the foreground or as a background shell, after the user approves the exact command. There is no shell parser on purpose: `argv` is an array, so pipes, redirections and substitutions cannot be smuggled into an approved command.
